The decomposition (2.13)
requires bounding the discretization error overllsteps. Through the
decomposition (4.2) of
Section4.1, the relevant quantity is the expected weighted error|(Δql,Δpl)|2,ℓw∞|(\Delta_{q}^{l},\Delta_{p}^{l})|_{2,\ell^{\infty}_{w}}. We bound it through the surrogate(E.33)|Δql|2,ℓ∞+a​|Δpl|2,ℓ∞,|\Delta_{q}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+\sqrt{a}|\Delta_{p}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}\,,

an upper bound for|(Δql,Δpl)|2,ℓw∞|(\Delta_{q}^{l},\Delta_{p}^{l})|_{2,\ell^{\infty}_{w}}since(E.34)|(Δql,Δpl)|2,ℓw∞=|(Δql+b​Δpl,a−b2​Δpl)|2,ℓ∞≤|Δql|2,ℓ∞+a​|Δpl|2,ℓ∞.|(\Delta_{q}^{l},\Delta_{p}^{l})|_{2,\ell^{\infty}_{w}}=\left|(\Delta_{q}^{l}+b\Delta_{p}^{l},\sqrt{a-b^{2}}\Delta_{p}^{l})\right|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}\leq|\Delta_{q}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+\sqrt{a}|\Delta_{p}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}\,.

To bound this surrogate, one could combine the single-step error of
PropositionE.4with the joint-space
contraction of CorollaryD.3. This is too
crude, as it treats the position and momentum errors together. Instead, we use a
Grönwall argument that tracks the two separately, mirroring theℓ2\ell^{2}analysis of[32].

## Proposition E.5(Refined statement of Proposition5.2).

For the(Δql,Δpl)(\Delta_{q}^{l},\Delta_{p}^{l})process with shared initial state(q∗,p∗)∼π⊗𝒩​(0,I)(q^{*},p^{*})\sim\pi\otimes\mathcal{N}(0,I), ifπ\pisatisfies
Assumption1.1andh≤1−η2​βh\leq\frac{1-\eta}{2\sqrt{\beta}},
then the accumulated discretization error overllsteps satisfies(E.35)|(Δql,Δpl)|2,ℓw∞≤|Δql|2,ℓ∞+a​|Δpl|2,ℓ∞≤exp⁡[(l−1)​h​(32​C5β+116​C6β3/2+β)]​El,|(\Delta_{q}^{l},\Delta_{p}^{l})|_{2,\ell^{\infty}_{w}}\leq|\Delta_{q}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+\sqrt{a}|\Delta_{p}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}\leq\exp\left[(l-1)h\Big(\tfrac{3}{2}\tfrac{C_{5}}{\sqrt{\beta}}+\tfrac{1}{16}\tfrac{C_{6}}{\beta^{3/2}}+\sqrt{\beta}\Big)\right]E_{l}\,,

whereC5,C6C_{5},C_{6}are the constants in
PropositionE.4and(E.36)El=(2764​C5​h3​l+278​h2​C5β​(1−η)+38​h4​C6β​(1−η))​log⁡(2​d).E_{l}=\left(\tfrac{27}{64}C_{5}h^{3}l+{\tfrac{27}{8}}\tfrac{h^{2}C_{5}}{\sqrt{\beta}(1-\eta)}+{\tfrac{3}{8}}\tfrac{h^{4}C_{6}}{\sqrt{\beta}(1-\eta)}\right)\sqrt{\log(2d)}\,.

## Proof of PropositionE.5.

By PropositionE.4, unrolling the position
error recurrence toΔq0=0\Delta_{q}^{0}=0gives(E.37)|Δql|2,ℓ∞\displaystyle|\Delta_{q}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}≤|Δql−1|2,ℓ∞+h​(h​C5​|Δql−1|2,ℓ∞+|Δpl−1|2,ℓ∞)+2764​C5​h3​log⁡(2​d)\displaystyle\leq|\Delta_{q}^{l-1}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+h(hC_{5}|\Delta_{q}^{l-1}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+|\Delta_{p}^{l-1}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}})+\tfrac{27}{64}C_{5}h^{3}\sqrt{\log(2d)}≤∑i=1l−1h​(h​C5​|Δqi|2,ℓ∞+|Δpi|2,ℓ∞)+2764​C5​l​h3​log⁡(2​d).\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}h(hC_{5}|\Delta_{q}^{i}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+|\Delta_{p}^{i}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}})+\tfrac{27}{64}C_{5}lh^{3}\sqrt{\log(2d)}\,.

Likewise, unrolling the momentum error recurrence and bounding the geometric sum
of the constant term by(1−η)−1(1-\eta)^{-1}yields(E.38)|Δpl|2,ℓ∞\displaystyle|\Delta_{p}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}≤(η+h22​C5)​|Δpl−1|2,ℓ∞+(h​C5+h34​C6)​|Δql−1|2,ℓ∞+|εp|2,ℓ∞\displaystyle\leq(\eta+\tfrac{h^{2}}{2}C_{5})|\Delta_{p}^{l-1}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+(hC_{5}+\tfrac{h^{3}}{4}C_{6})|\Delta_{q}^{l-1}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon_{p}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}≤∑i=1l−1ηi−1​h22​C5​|Δpl−i|2,ℓ∞+∑i=1l−1ηi−1​(h​C5+h3​C64)​|Δql−i|2,ℓ∞+11−η​|εp|2,ℓ∞.\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}\eta^{i-1}\tfrac{h^{2}}{2}C_{5}|\Delta_{p}^{l-i}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}\eta^{i-1}(hC_{5}+\tfrac{h^{3}C_{6}}{4})|\Delta_{q}^{l-i}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+\tfrac{1}{1-\eta}|\varepsilon_{p}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}\,.

Combining the two, withh≤1/(2​β)h\leq 1/(2\sqrt{\beta})andη≤1\eta\leq 1, we obtain(E.39)|Δql|2,ℓ∞+a​|Δpl|2,ℓ∞≤∑i=1l−1h​(32​C5β+116​C6β3/2+β)​(|Δql−i|2,ℓ∞+a​|Δpl−i|2,ℓ∞)+El.|\Delta_{q}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+\sqrt{a}|\Delta_{p}^{l}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}h\Big(\tfrac{3}{2}\tfrac{C_{5}}{\sqrt{\beta}}+\tfrac{1}{16}\tfrac{C_{6}}{\beta^{3/2}}+\sqrt{\beta}\Big)(|\Delta_{q}^{l-i}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}}+\sqrt{a}|\Delta_{p}^{l-i}|_{2,\ell^{\infty}})+E_{l}\,.

Applying Grönwall’s inequality leads to (E.35).
∎

## Appendix FSampling Bias Bounds

Under the framework of Section2, we now combine the
propagator norm estimates with the discretization error to track the sampling
error across steps, and obtain the bias bounds in the limit of infinitely many
steps.

## F.1.HMC

We prove the iterativeW2,ℓ∞W_{2,\ell^{\infty}}bounds of
Propositions2.1and2.2, from which the HMC cases of
Theorems1.4and1.6follow by iterating to
the stationary limit.
We begin with the weak interaction case.

## Proof of Proposition2.1.

Following the coupling and one-step split (2.4) into the
contraction term(a)(a)and discretization term(b)(b), we bound each.
